# AXM Universal Creation Map v0.1

Recovered standalone data view generated from the canonical registry.
The canonical machine-readable source is `registry/master_registry.json`.
Current standalone AXM rules take precedence over older Workshop-era architecture notes.

## Inventory

- 1000 candidate atom classes
- 750 candidate component classes
- 415 candidate organ classes
- 2165 total candidate classes
- 100 implementation-kernel records

This is an extensible research baseline, not a claim of universal completeness.

# Atoms

## 00-foundation | Universal foundation and metadata

### AXM-00-FOUNDATION-A-001-identifier | identifier

Smallest independently addressable identifier unit in universal foundation and metadata, intended to be typed, referenced, transformed or validated without requiring a whole finished asset.

- Source basis: cross-standard
- Sources: SRC-JSONSCHEMA, SRC-RDF, SRC-PROV, SRC-SPDX
- Registry status: candidate
- Maturity: research-seed
